It uses kernel fusion + recomputation: during forward pass, only the last state is stored; during backward pass, the scan is recomputed from stored intermediate values. This is essential for memory efficiency with time-varying parameters.\", \"details\": \"The custom kernel implements a parallel associative scan where \\u0394, A, B, C are all input-dependent. Includes the hardware-aware parallel scan (associative scan) implementation in pure PyTorch (for CPU/debug) and a stub for the custom CUDA kernel.\", \"mamba_block.py - Mamba block: single homogeneous block combining the selective SSM with gated MLP. Uses SiLU activation, expands hidden dimension by a factor (default 2), and applies the SSM to the gated branch. No LayerNorm inside the block.\", \"mamba_mixer.py - MambaMixer (the full Mamba layer): stacks multiple Mamba blocks with residual connections, RMSNorm before each block, and optional final normalization. Handles the overall sequence transformation.\", \"mamba_lm.py - Mamba language model head: wraps MambaMixer with an embedding layer and output LM head (tied weights optional). Supports causal language modeling.\", \"selective_copying.py - Synthetic task: Selective Copying. Generates sequences with tokens to copy and distractor tokens.
